# Agents · S · part 20

954 entries, surface-discovery-agent to synthesis-critic. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **surface-discovery-agent**: Runs bounded normal surface-discovery — subdomain enum, live hosts, archived/crawled URLs, nuclei, JS/JWT extraction — and produces attack_surface.json ([vmihalis/hacker-bob](https://github.com/vmihalis/hacker-bob/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/surface-discovery-agent.md) · ★98)

- **surface-reviewer**: Review a plan's Security & Cost Surface against seven question-shaped rows: untrusted input → LLM, external mutation partial-failure space, endpoint surface (authz / rate / state / cross-tenant), audit-log fidelity, state-machine walkthroughs with degradation signals, convention enforcement level, and client-side state. - **surface-reviewer**: Use proactively after editing a skill, agent card, hook, rule file, settings.json or an AGENTS.md section. Takes a SET of agent-surface units, extracts every claim one unit makes about another ("hook X refuses Y", "tool Z takes --f", "see §N", "the one home is", "agent may write P") and tests each against the unit it names (reads it, runs --help, pipes a sample payload into the hook, WebFetch for a host-doc claim); flags one-home collisions and pointer cycles. Writes a ≤ 60-line report to scratch/, returns VERDICT + CLAIM rows. "retest &lt;old report&gt;" = before/after. Read-only on the repo. ([brycejmurrin/f1-game](https://github.com/brycejmurrin/f1-game/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/surface-reviewer.md))

([mharini2205/act_first](https://github.com/mharini2205/act_first/blob/HEAD/plugins/act-first/agents/surface-scout.md))

- **SURFACE_CONTRADICTIONS_DONT_PICK_SIDES_v1**: When two sources disagree, **say so out loud**. Never silently pick one side. - **surgeon**: Operate-fleet self-healing fixer. Receives a triage record from Diagnostician, spawns into a fresh git worktree, coordinates Cartographer (re-inspect), the relevant Forger (regenerate), and Tester (regression), and opens a PR with the fix. Routes through HITL via Concierge when the fix touches more workflows than `policy.operate.surgeon.max_workflows_touched_without_hitl`. Use this agent when Diagnostician dispatches with `auto_fix: true` or for HITL fixes. ([mlbrilliance/uipath-for-coding-agents](https://github.com/mlbrilliance/uipath-for-coding-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/surgeon.md))
- **surgeon**: Diagnoses Django failures. Use proactively, before answering, whenever the developer reports something broken or asks why it fails, even when the cause looks obvious from the code: a traceback, a 500, a failing test, a migration that will not apply, a view returning the wrong data, a template rendering nothing, any "it worked yesterday". Finds the actual cause before anything is changed. Trigger phrases: this is broken, traceback, error, failing test, why is this happening, 500, IntegrityError, migration conflict, it worked yesterday, debug this, find out why. ([philchurch77/crew](https://github.com/philchurch77/crew/blob/HEAD/plugins/crew/agents/surgeon.md))

- **swarm-turbo-recon**: Fast, cheap structural reconnaissance for the first wave of a multi-agent swarm. Returns a compact map, never an analysis. ([renanfita/swarm-turbo-skill](https://github.com/renanfita/swarm-turbo-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-turbo-recon.md))
- **swarm-turbo-synthesizer**: Merges the output of many parallel agents into one deduplicated, conflict-resolved, ranked and cited report. Use it as the final stage of a swarm when there are enough agent outputs that merging them in the main conversation would dominate its context. Read-only. ([renanfita/swarm-turbo-skill](https://github.com/renanfita/swarm-turbo-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-turbo-synthesizer.md))
- **swarm-turbo-verifier**: Adversarial verifier for multi-agent swarm findings. Use it to independently check a claim another agent produced - a bug, a vulnerability, a performance problem, a factual assertion - before it reaches a report. It tries to refute the claim and defaults to refuted when the evidence does not hold. Never modifies anything; may run non-mutating commands to reproduce. ([renanfita/swarm-turbo-skill](https://github.com/renanfita/swarm-turbo-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-turbo-verifier.md))
- **swarm-validator**: Post-merge validator. Verifies merged work actually helped — runs relevant checks after each merge type. Catches regressions. Creates priority issues for failures. ([EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp](https://github.com/EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp/blob/HEAD/docs/handoff/swarm-pack/agents/swarm-validator.md) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **swarm-worker**: Execute a single swarm task in an isolated git worktree. Receive a fully-specified recipe from the main context — a scenario recipe plus an implementation contract — then run `Skill(scenario)` followed by `Skill(implement)` and report JSON status. Make no design decisions and do not expand scope. Invoked exclusively by `/swarm-dispatch`; never elsewhere. ([friedbotstudio/baseline](https://github.com/friedbotstudio/baseline/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/swarm-worker.md) · ★14)
- **swarm-worker**: Autonomous task executor for devloop:run-swarm. Implements a single plan task with fresh context. Do not use for interactive work. Use when: Orchestrator is running swarm mode and needs to parallelize independent plan tasks. Do NOT use when: Interactive work is needed, or for single-task execution (just do the work directly). &lt;example&gt; Context: The orchestrator is running devloop:run-swarm and needs to execute a plan task assistant: "I'll spawn a swarm-worker to implement this task with fresh context." &lt;/example&gt; ([Zate/cc-plugins](https://github.com/Zate/cc-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/devloop/agents/swarm-worker.md) · ★10)
- **swarm-worker**: one swarm worker for a slice or race arm. ([edheltzel/Do-Pstack](https://github.com/edheltzel/Do-Pstack/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-worker.md) · ★2)
- **swarm-worker-contracts**: Defines the strict JSON schemas for all Swarm dispatch and result payloads. Every `WorkerRequest` and `WorkerResult` MUST conform to these schemas. Used by: `supervisor-agent`, `swarm_dispatcher.js`, `/swarm` workflow. ([Harmitx7/tribunal-kit](https://github.com/Harmitx7/tribunal-kit/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/swarm-worker-contracts.md) · ★5)
- **swarm-worker-haiku**: Use this agent for simple swarm tasks: deletions, renames, config changes, boilerplate generation. &lt;example&gt; Context: Swarm orchestrator assigns task "Delete old file src/old-code.ts" user: "Execute task 3.1: Delete file" assistant: "[Spawns swarm-worker-haiku to handle deletion]" &lt;commentary&gt; Simple task, no complex logic - haiku is sufficient. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([amoscicki/aromatt](https://github.com/amoscicki/aromatt/blob/HEAD/swarm/agents/swarm-worker-haiku.md) · 2 copies)
- **swarm-worker-kit**: **Purpose:** Execute assigned implementation tasks as part of a parallel swarm. Implementation only — no quality checks, no commits, no PRs. ([hamiltonroad/checked-out](https://github.com/hamiltonroad/checked-out/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/swarm-worker-kit.md))
- **swarm-worker-opus**: Use this agent for standard swarm tasks: feature implementation, integration, bug fixes, service creation. &lt;example&gt; Context: Swarm orchestrator assigns task "Implement booking validation service" user: "Execute task 2.3: Create validation service" assistant: "[Spawns swarm-worker-opus for implementation]" &lt;commentary&gt; Standard implementation task requiring code understanding - opus handles this. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([amoscicki/aromatt](https://github.com/amoscicki/aromatt/blob/HEAD/swarm/agents/swarm-worker-opus.md) · 2 copies)
- **swarm-worker-registry**: Maps task types and domain keywords to the correct specialist Worker agents. Used by `supervisor-agent` to select the correct `agent` field in every `WorkerRequest`. All agents listed here MUST exist as `.md` files in `.agent/agents/`. ([Harmitx7/tribunal-kit](https://github.com/Harmitx7/tribunal-kit/blob/HEAD/.agent/agents/swarm-worker-registry.md) · ★5)
- **swarm-worker-ultrathink**: Use this agent for complex swarm tasks: architecture decisions, algorithms, edge cases, schema interpreters. &lt;example&gt; Context: Swarm orchestrator assigns task "Implement JSON Schema to Effect Schema interpreter" user: "Execute task 3.3: Create restoreSchema function" assistant: "[Spawns swarm-worker-ultrathink for complex algorithm]" &lt;commentary&gt; Complex interpreter with recursive logic and edge cases - ultrathink required. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([amoscicki/aromatt](https://github.com/amoscicki/aromatt/blob/HEAD/swarm/agents/swarm-worker-ultrathink.md) · 2 copies)
- **swarm-workers**: pstack swarm worker. Same posture as the pstack role of this name. Shipped effort is frontmatter `effort`. Setup may overlay via ~/.grok/roles/pstack:swarm-workers.toml. ([tommy-ca/pstack](https://github.com/tommy-ca/pstack/blob/HEAD/agents/swarm-workers.md))
- **swarmfish-entity-extractor**: Extract entities and relationships from seed content for SwarmFish simulations. Use when building a knowledge graph from text. ([sabirmgd/swarmfish](https://github.com/sabirmgd/swarmfish/blob/HEAD/agents/entity-extractor.md) · ★1)
- **swarmfish-interviewer**: Conduct in-character interviews with SwarmFish simulation agents. Responds as the agent based on their persona and simulation history. ([sabirmgd/swarmfish](https://github.com/sabirmgd/swarmfish/blob/HEAD/agents/interviewer.md) · ★1)
- **swarmfish-persona-generator**: Generate detailed agent personas from entities for SwarmFish simulations. Creates MBTI, background, goals, biases, and system prompts. ([sabirmgd/swarmfish](https://github.com/sabirmgd/swarmfish/blob/HEAD/agents/persona-generator.md) · ★1)
- **swarmfish-reporter**: Generate comprehensive prediction reports from SwarmFish simulation data. Synthesizes agent actions, sentiment evolution, and graph metrics into evidence-based predictions. ([sabirmgd/swarmfish](https://github.com/sabirmgd/swarmfish/blob/HEAD/agents/reporter.md) · ★1)
- **swarmfish-simulator**: Execute individual agent turns in SwarmFish simulation rounds. Stays in character and produces actions based on persona and context. ([sabirmgd/swarmfish](https://github.com/sabirmgd/swarmfish/blob/HEAD/agents/simulator.md) · ★1)
- **swarmlord**: Sonnet cross-faction expansion scout. Given a rule shape that works (an effect type, condition, or encoding pattern), finds abilities in OTHER factions coverable by the same shape — via embeddings clustering/candidates plus keyword sweeps over the prose store — to widen coverage per work cycle. Use for "where else does &lt;shape&gt; apply?", "find the family for this mechanic". Prompt must include the shape and an example ability_id. Returns a single JSON object as final message. ([wn-mitch/40kdc-data](https://github.com/wn-mitch/40kdc-data/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/swarmlord.md) · ★30)
- **swat-fixer**: 🪰 Swat Reviewer fixer. Addresses the open swat-reviewer comments on a PR (applying their suggested fixes) in the worktree, re-verifies, and reports. Spawned by /handle-it's Phase-6 review⇄fix loop. Never commits or pushes. ([swatkinson/swatkinson-cc-toolkit](https://github.com/swatkinson/swatkinson-cc-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/swatkinson-toolkit/agents/swat-fixer.md))
- **swat-reviewer**: 🪰 Swat Reviewer code reviewer. Does ONE in-house review pass over a PR's diff (no external code-review/simplify skill), posts P#-tagged inline comments (with a Quality/Spec facet tag) WITH suggested fixes, resolves the review threads it confirms fixed, and posts/updates the single rating comment scoring three facets — Code Quality, Spec. Adherence, Risk and Complexity (each 0–5). Spawned by the /swat-review skill (one pass per invocation). Posts/resolves comments only — never edits code, commits, or pushes. ([swatkinson/swatkinson-cc-toolkit](https://github.com/swatkinson/swatkinson-cc-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/swatkinson-toolkit/agents/swat-reviewer.md))
- **swd-reviewer**: Use this agent when reviewing charts, visualizations, or their accompanying prose for adherence to Storytelling with Data (SWD) methodology. This agent focuses exclusively on visual communication quality — it does not check data accuracy (notebook-qa) or logical rigor (critic).
